//! Asset/reference renames transport cells, homes and lifetime evidence.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — asset identity is preserved; no upload, re-claim or generated placeholder.
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
type Maps=BTreeMap<String,BTreeMap<String,String>>;
fn refusal(message:impl Into<String>)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:message.into()}}
fn replace_keys<T>(fields:BTreeMap<String,T>,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->BTreeMap<String,T> {fields.into_iter().map(|(name,value)|(map.get(&name).cloned().unwrap_or(name),value)).collect()}
fn table(table:&mut Table,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){
    table.columns=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.columns),map);
    table.defaults=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.defaults),map);
    table.column_reads=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.column_reads),map);
    table.on_delete=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.on_delete),map);
    table.written_by=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.written_by),map);
    fn term(t:&mut crate::schema::RuleTerm,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){use crate::schema::RuleTerm::*;if let Field(c)|OldField(c)|NextField(c)|Reference{column:c,..}|ReferenceChain{column:c,..}=t {if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}}}
    fn rule(r:&mut crate::schema::RuleExpr,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;match r {Eq(a,b)|Ne(a,b)|Compare(_,a,b)=>{term(a,map);term(b,map)},And(xs)|Or(xs)=>for x in xs{rule(x,map)},Not(r)=>rule(r,map),Exists{key,equalities,..}|Created{key,equalities,..}=>{for t in key{term(t,map)}for(_,t)in equalities{term(t,map)}},_=>()}}
    for r in [&mut table.rules.read,&mut table.rules.insert,&mut table.rules.update,&mut table.rules.delete]{rule(r,map)}
    for r in table.column_reads.values_mut(){rule(r,map)}
    for index in table.indexes.values_mut(){for component in &mut index.components {if let crate::schema::IndexComponent::Column(c)=component {if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}}}}
    if let Some(sync)=&mut table.sync {
        for audience in std::iter::once(&mut sync.audience).chain(sync.also.iter_mut().map(|a|&mut a.audience)) {
            match audience {crate::schema::Audience::Column(c)=>{if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}},crate::schema::Audience::Target{prefix,..}=>for c in prefix {if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}},_=>()}
        }
    }
    for c in &mut table.constraints {match c{
        crate::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields}|crate::schema::Constraint::Cap{by:fields,..}=>for f in fields{if let Some(new)=map.get(f){*f=new.clone();}},
        crate::schema::Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..}=>for t in key{term(t,map)},
        _=>(),
    }}
}
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — comparison uses the same typed identity codec as activation.
impl Schema {
    /// Compare schema authority using declared physical column identities.
    pub fn with_column_names(&self,maps:&BTreeMap<String,BTreeMap<String,String>>)->Self{schema(self.clone(),maps)}
}
pub(super) fn schema(mut schema:Schema,maps:&Maps)->Schema {
    for(name,map)in maps {if let Some(t)=schema.tables.get_mut(name){table(t,map);}}
    for e in &mut schema.extractions {
        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.child){e.reference=map.get(&e.reference).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||e.reference.clone());e.fields=std::mem::take(&mut e.fields).into_iter().map(|(dest,source)|(map.get(&dest).cloned().unwrap_or(dest),source)).collect();}
        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.parent){for source in e.fields.values_mut(){if let Some(new)=map.get(source){*source=new.clone();}}}
    }schema
}
pub(super) fn prepare(conn:&Connection,next:&Schema,catalog:&BTreeMap<String,i64>,renamed_tables:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<Maps,RuntimeError>{
    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_asset_column_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    if !exists && next.asset_renames.is_empty(){return Ok(Maps::new())}
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _asset_column_renames(t INTEGER NOT NULL,name TEXT NOT NULL,origin TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    let mut maps=Maps::new();
    for (name,current) in &next.tables {
        let Some(t)=catalog.get(name) else {continue};
        let mut aliases:BTreeMap<String,String>=conn.prepare("SELECT name,origin FROM _asset_column_renames WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        for (new,old) in next.asset_renames.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            if old==new || current.columns.contains_key(old) || !current.columns.get(new).is_some_and(asset){return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} requires one predecessor-only source and one asset destination")))}
            let root=aliases.get(old).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||old.clone());
            if aliases.get(new).is_some_and(|r|r!=&root){return Err(refusal("asset rename cannot merge independent column lifetimes"))}
            for c in [old,new] {aliases.insert(c.clone(),root.clone());conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _asset_column_renames VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,c,root])?;}
        }
        let mut selected=BTreeMap::new();for c in current.columns.keys(){if let Some(root)=aliases.get(c){if selected.insert(root.clone(),c.clone()).is_some(){return Err(refusal("two names for the same asset column cannot be active together"))}}}
        let map:BTreeMap<_,_>=aliases.into_iter().filter_map(|(old,root)|selected.get(&root).filter(|new|**new!=old).map(|new|(old,new.clone()))).collect();
        for(old,new)in &map {
            let prior:Option<String>=conn.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
            if let Some(prior)=prior {
                let prior:Schema=serde_json::from_str(&prior).map_err(|e|refusal(e.to_string()))?;
                let prior=prior.with_table_names(renamed_tables)?;
                if let Some(before)=prior.table(name).filter(|t|t.columns.contains_key(old)){
                    let source=&before.columns[old];let destination=&current.columns[new];
                    if reference(source)||reference(destination){
                        if source!=destination||before.on_delete.get(old)!=current.on_delete.get(new)||before.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*old)||current.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*new){return Err(refusal("reference rename requires identical nominal type, deletion policy and uncaptured ownership in both directions"))}
                    }
                }
            }
            for rows in ["r","_dormant_rows","_dormant_narrow_rows"] {
                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
                let a=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(old).unwrap());let b=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(new).unwrap());
                let conflict:bool=conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {rows} WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL AND json_type(data,?3) IS NOT NULL AND json_extract(data,?2)!=json_extract(data,?3))"),params![t,a,b],|r|r.get(0))?;
                if conflict{return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} has a conflicting physical destination")))}
                conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET data=json_remove(json_set(data,?3,json_extract(data,?2)),?2) WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL"),params![t,a,b])?;
            }
            conn.execute("UPDATE _assets SET home_column=?3 WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_column=?2",params![name,old,new])?;
            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_refs")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_refs SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_values")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_columns")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_columns SET column_name=?3 WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![name,old,new])?;}
        }
        if !map.is_empty(){
            for rows in ["_dormant_narrow_rows","_dormant_tables"] {
                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
                let condition=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{"table_name=?1"}else{"t=?1"};
                let value=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{rusqlite::types::Value::Text(name.clone())}else{rusqlite::types::Value::Integer(*t)};
                let definitions:Vec<String>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT DISTINCT definition FROM {rows} WHERE {condition}"))?.query_map([&value],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
                for text in definitions {let mut definition:Table=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;table(&mut definition,&map);conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET definition=?2 WHERE {condition} AND definition=?3"),params![value,canonical_json(&definition),text])?;}
            }
            maps.insert(name.clone(),map);
        }
    }
    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version'",[super::table_renames::VERSION])?;
    Ok(maps)
}

//! Table names may change; numeric row, reference and receipt lifetimes do not.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — renames are identity moves, never row copies or merges.
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
pub(super) const VERSION:&str="snapback4-release-store-9";
fn refuse(s:impl Into<String>)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:s.into()}}

// Only structural table identifiers change. Literal values, column names,
// operation names and authored defaults are never table identifiers.
fn identifiers(value:&mut serde_json::Value,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>) {
    match value {
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs {identifiers(x,map)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>for (key,x) in fields {
            if key=="Literal" || key=="defaults" {continue;}
            if matches!(key.as_str(),"table"|"parent") {if let Some(name)=x.as_str().and_then(|s|map.get(s)){*x=serde_json::Value::String(name.clone());continue;}}
            identifiers(x,map);
        },_=>()
    }
}
fn mapped(name:&str,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->String {map.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())}
fn remap<T:serde::Serialize+serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(value:&T,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<T,RuntimeError> {
    let mut json=serde_json::to_value(value).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;identifiers(&mut json,map);
    serde_json::from_value(json).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))
}
pub(crate) fn schema(mut schema:Schema,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<Schema,RuntimeError> {
    if map.is_empty(){return Ok(schema)}
    schema.tables=std::mem::take(&mut schema.tables).into_iter().map(|(name,table)|Ok((mapped(&name,map),remap(&table,map)?))).collect::<Result<_,RuntimeError>>()?;
    for m in &mut schema.maintains {*m=remap(m,map)?;m.source=mapped(&m.source,map);m.target=mapped(&m.target,map);}
    for f in &mut schema.backfills {fill(f,map,false)?;}
    for f in &mut schema.downfills {fill(f,map,true)?;}
    for e in &mut schema.extractions {e.child=mapped(&e.child,map);}
    // Remaining table-bearing structural metadata is normalized through the
    // same typed codec; maintains' source/target are handled explicitly above.
    // These maps contain column identifiers, including arbitrary keys such as
    // "parent". Normalize their typed table slots, never their column strings.
    let mut extractions=std::mem::take(&mut schema.extractions);
    for e in &mut extractions {e.parent=mapped(&e.parent,map);}
    let columns=std::mem::take(&mut schema.asset_renames).into_iter().map(|(name,columns)|(mapped(&name,map),columns)).collect();
    schema.table_renames.clear();schema=remap(&schema,map)?;
    schema.extractions=extractions;schema.asset_renames=columns;
    Ok(schema)
}
fn fill(f:&mut crate::schema::backfill::Backfill,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>,down:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    *f=remap(f,map)?;
    f.program.name=format!("{}{}{}",if down{"down"}else{"backfill"},f.table,f.name);Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn prepare(conn:&Connection,next:&Schema,catalog:&mut BTreeMap<String,i64>)->Result<BTreeMap<String,String>,RuntimeError> {
    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_table_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    if !exists && next.table_renames.is_empty(){return Ok(BTreeMap::new())}
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _table_renames(name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,origin TEXT NOT NULL) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;

    for (new,old) in &next.table_renames {
        if new==old || !next.tables.contains_key(new) || next.tables.contains_key(old){return Err(refuse("table rename requires a distinct predecessor-only source"))}
        let origin=names.get(old).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||old.clone());
        if names.get(new).is_some_and(|root|root!=&origin){return Err(refuse(format!("table rename {old} → {new} conflicts with an independent retained identity")))}
        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(catalog.get(old),catalog.get(new)) {if a!=b{return Err(refuse(format!("table rename {old} → {new} cannot merge existing table lifetimes")))}}
        for name in [old,new] {names.insert(name.clone(),origin.clone());conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _table_renames VALUES(?1,?2)",params![name,origin])?;}
    }
    let mut selected=BTreeMap::<String,String>::new();
    for name in next.tables.keys() {if let Some(root)=names.get(name) {if let Some(other)=selected.insert(root.clone(),name.clone()){return Err(refuse(format!("table aliases {other} and {name} cannot be active together")))}}}
    let map:BTreeMap<String,String>=names.iter().filter_map(|(name,root)|selected.get(root).filter(|new|*new!=name).map(|new|(name.clone(),new.clone()))).collect();
    for (old,new) in &map {
        if !catalog.contains_key(old){continue;}

        let entries:Vec<_>=catalog.iter().filter(|(key,_)|*key==old || key.starts_with(&format!("{old}."))).map(|(k,v)|(k.clone(),*v)).collect();

    For { name: String, domain: Expr, body: Vec<Stmt>, site: Site },
    /// `break`: the enclosing `for` or `chunk` ends.
    Break { site: Site },
    Continue { site: Site },
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct IndexDecl {
    pub name: String,
    /// The columns, in order, the `words(..)` component left out.
    pub columns: Vec<String>,
    pub unique: bool,
    pub refusal: Option<(String, Option<String>)>,
    /// `words(a, b)`: its position among the components (the number of
    /// columns before it) and the text columns it tokenizes. An index with
    /// one serves `matching` reads whose keys are exactly those columns,
    /// and nothing else.
    pub words: Option<(usize, Vec<String>)>,
    pub computed: std::collections::BTreeMap<String, Expr>,
    pub site: Site,
}

impl IndexDecl {
    /// Whether the index serves ordinary prefix reads and probes.
    pub fn is_plain(&self) -> bool {
        self.words.is_none() && self.computed.is_empty()
    }

    /// Whether a `matching` read with this many keys reads this index.
    pub fn searches_after(&self, keys: usize) -> bool {
        self.words.as_ref().is_some_and(|(position, _)| *position == keys)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub enum Effect {
    Read,
    Insert,
    Update,
    Delete,
}

impl Effect {
    pub fn word(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Read => "read",
            Self::Insert => "insert",
            Self::Update => "update",
            Self::Delete => "delete",
}


#[test]
fn invariant_apps_keep_ordinary_rename_split_and_required_field_upgrades() {
    // Small independent shapes from wave-25 002/004/020/024/036/044.
    // Real row images, an uploaded image and default rollback exercise storage,
    // rather than relying on a successful check of empty source alone.
    let base="table items:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  label: text 1..100\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nquery all():\n  return items first 10 by .id\nmutation create(label: text 1..100):\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,label}\ninvariant items.read only .owner = viewer\n";
    let rename=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  title: text 1..100 renamed from label read <- .owner = viewer")
        .replace("create(label: text 1..100)","create(title: text 1..100 alias label)")
        .replace("{owner:viewer,label}","{owner:viewer,title}")
        +"invariant items.title read only .owner = viewer\n";
    let split=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  invitation: text 1..100\n  envelope: text 1..100")
        .replace("create(label: text 1..100)","create(invitation: text 1..100, envelope: text 1..100)")
        .replace("{owner:viewer,label}","{owner:viewer,invitation,envelope}")
        +"backfill items invitationFromLabel(label: text 1..100): {invitation:label}\nbackfill items envelopeFromLabel(label: text 1..100): {envelope:label}\ndown items oldLabel(invitation: text 1..100): {label:invitation}\ninvariant items.invitation read only .owner = viewer\n";
    let reference=base.replace("table items:","table records renamed from items:")
        .replace("return items","return records").replace("insert items","insert records")
        .replace("invariant items.","invariant records.")
        +"table attachments:\n  parent: records\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  online only\n";
    let asset_old=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  label: text 1..100\n  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4")
        .replace("mutation create(label: text 1..100):\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,label}\n","");
    let asset_split=asset_old.replace("  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4\n","")
        +"table surveys:\n  parent: items\n  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  online only\nextract surveys from items via parent: {photos:photos}\n";
    let alias=reference.replace("mutation create(label:","mutation openRecord(title:")
        .replace("text 1..100):\n  return insert records {owner:viewer,label}","text 1..100 alias label):\n  return insert records {owner:viewer,label:title}")
        +"alias create = openRecord\n";
    let required=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  label: text 1..100\n  priority: int 1..9 default 3");
    for(name,old,next,row)in [
        ("column rename",base.to_owned(),rename,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("split with truthful down",base.to_owned(),split,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("nominal rename and child reference",base.to_owned(),reference,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("required image to child",asset_old,asset_split,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original","photos":"fixture-image"})),
        ("table operation and parameter aliases",base.to_owned(),alias,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("add required with authored default",base.to_owned(),required,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
    ] {
        let app=App::new();app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);app.seed(&old,vec![row]);
        app.source(&next);
        let deployed=app.run(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
        assert!(deployed.status.success(),"{name}: {}",text(&deployed));
        let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0,"{name}: {preview}");
        let rollback=app.run(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);
        assert!(rollback.status.success(),"{name}: {}",text(&rollback));
        assert_eq!(app.count(),1,"{name}");
        let rows=app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(rows["rows"][0][0],"original","{name}: {rows}");
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
    }
}

                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&index.site, format!("duplicate index {}.{}", table.name, index.name), "name one ordering explicitly"));
                        ok = false;

                        }

            } else if issue.message.contains("maintained column") || issue.message.contains("guide maintains") {
                diagnostics.push(super::columns::refusal(&site, issue.message));

                if let Some(name) = issue.path.strip_prefix("tables.").and_then(|path| path.strip_suffix(".sync"))
                    && let Some(maintain) = catalog.maintains.get(name)
                    && let Some(context) = super::audience_diagnostics::public_conflict(&schema, maintain,
                        maintain.read.as_ref().map_or(&maintain.site, |read| &read.site), "E_SYNC") {
                    diagnostics.push(context);
                    continue;
                }
                let rewrite = if issue.message.contains("needs required group keys") { "remove `?` from every content and audience group column named by this sync" } else { "make the sync audience exactly match the table's read rule" };
                diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&site, "E_SYNC", issue.message, format!("{rewrite}; for staff plus member audiences see: guide rule-recipes offline-roster")).guide("sync"));
            } else {
                diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&site, "E_SCHEMA_CONSTRAINT", issue.message, "fix the declaration named by this message").guide("indexes"));
            }
        }
        diagnostics.extend(grants.diagnostics(&schema, catalog));
    }
    schema
}

/// Bind one table. A refused line becomes a diagnostic and a `deny` (or an
/// absent sync), so one mistake does not cascade into every reader.
fn bind_table(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Table {
    let decl = &info.decl;
    let mut columns = BTreeMap::new();
    columns.insert("id".to_owned(), core::ColumnType::Id);
    for column in &decl.columns {
        if let TypeKind::Ref { table } = &column.type_.kind
            && !catalog.tables.contains_key(table)
        {
            diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site, format!("column {}.{} references unknown table {table}", decl.name, column.name), name_rewrite(table, catalog.tables.keys().map(String::as_str), Some("declare the table, or use id for an opaque identity"))));
        }
        columns.insert(column.name.clone(), scalar_to_column(&column.type_));
    }
    let mut recorded = BTreeMap::new();
    for (path, kind) in &info.recorded {
        let (reference, column) = path.split_once('.').expect("admitted path");
        let TypeKind::Ref { table } = &info.columns[reference].kind else { unreachable!("admitted reference") };
        recorded.insert(path.clone(), core::RecordedField { reference: reference.into(), table: table.clone(), column: column.into() });
        columns.insert(path.clone(), scalar_to_column(kind));
    }
    columns.extend(info.captures.iter().map(|(name, ty)| (name.clone(), scalar_to_column(ty))));
    let mut indexes = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut constraints = Vec::new();
    for index in &info.indexes {
        let mut bound = core::Index::of(&index.columns.iter().map(String::as_str).collect::<Vec<_>>(), index.unique);
        for (position, column) in index.columns.iter().enumerate() {
            if let Some(expr) = index.computed.get(column) {
                match super::time_indexes::key(&decl.name, &info.columns, column, expr) {
                    Ok(key) => bound.components[position] = core::IndexComponent::Calendar(key),
                    Err(_) => {} // The catalog has already located this refusal.
                }
            }
        }
        if let Some((position, fields)) = &index.words {
            bound.components.insert(*position, core::IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields: fields.clone(), contract: serde_json::Value::String(snapback4_core::words::CONTRACT.to_owned()) });
        }
        indexes.insert(index.name.clone(), bound);
        if index.unique && index.name != "byId" {
            constraints.push(Constraint::Unique { index: index.name.clone() });
        }
    }
    let mut rules = Rules::deny();
    for effect in [Effect::Read, Effect::Insert, Effect::Update, Effect::Delete] {
        let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == effect);
        let bound = match (effect, &decl.public, rule) {
            (Effect::Read, Some((reason, _)), Some(rule)) => {
                diagnostics.push(unknown(&rule.site, format!("table {} cannot combine public with an explicit read rule", decl.name), format!("remove the read line, or remove `public '{reason}'`")));
                RuleExpr::Deny

    94	        return Bridge{diagnostics:complete.diagnostics.into_iter().chain(guarded.diagnostics).collect(),..guarded};
    95	    }
    96	    let guarded=loosen_guarded(target,current);
    97	    if !guarded.diagnostics.is_empty(){
    98	        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut false);
    99	        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty(){return complete;}
   100	        let current_bridge=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
   101	        if current_bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(){return current_bridge;}
   102	    }
   103	    guarded
   104	}

    65	pub(super) fn guarded(target:&[Source],current:&[Source])->Bridge {
    66	    let normalized=match alpha(target,current) {
    67	        Ok(sources)=>sources,
    68	        Err(error)=>return refused(current,&error.reason),
    69	    };
    70	    let current=normalized.as_deref().unwrap_or(current);
    71	    let old=crate::compile(target);let next=crate::compile(current);
    72	    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty()||!next.diagnostics.is_empty(){return refused(current,"both authored sources must compile before preparing a guarded view");}
    73	    if !table_contracts(&old,&next,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow needs every predecessor table and required/defaulted field; author a truthful reverse projection or compatible guarded source");}
    74	    let mut sources=current.to_vec();let mut changes=vec!["guarded loosened view: retained the complete current schema, defaults, helpers, programs, jobs, aliases and independent intent; omitted nullable default-free predecessor fields are absent results, never invented values; review current results in the old UI".into()];
    75	    if normalized.is_some(){changes.push("declared table identities: complete current guarded workflow retained under predecessor spellings, without rewriting business strings or weakening authority".into());}

   135	fn endpoint_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,pause_removed_writes:bool)->bool {
   136	    old.programs.iter().all(|p|new.programs.iter().find(|n|n.program.name==p.program.name)
   137	        .map_or_else(||pause_removed_writes&&matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action),|n|endpoint_contract(&p.program,&n.program)))
   138	}
   139	pub(super) fn endpoint_contract(old:&snapback4_core::Program,new:&snapback4_core::Program)->bool {
   140	    if old.kind!=new.kind{return false;}
   141	    let mut positions=BTreeSet::new();
   142	    for(name,kind)in &old.args {
   143	        let Some((target,next))=new.argument(name)else{return false};
   144	        if !accepts(kind,next)||!positions.insert(target){return false;}
   145	        if !old.arg_aliases.iter().filter(|(_,owner)|*owner==name).all(|(alias,_)|new.argument(alias).is_some_and(|(canonical,next)|canonical==target&&accepts(kind,next))){return false;}
   146	    }
        match table.indexes.get(BY_ID) {
            Some(index) if index.is_plain() && index.unique && index.columns() == ["id"] => {}
            _ => issue(&mut issues, format!("{path}.indexes.{BY_ID}"), "every table has the intrinsic unique byId index".into()),
        }
        for (name, index) in &table.indexes {
            let ipath = format!("{path}.indexes.{name}");
            if index.components.is_empty() || index.components.len() > 8 {
                issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), "declare between 1 and 8 components".into());
            }
            let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
            let mut words = 0;
            for component in &index.components {
                match component {
                    IndexComponent::Column(column) => {
                        if !seen.insert(column) {
                            issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), format!("duplicate column {column}"));
                        }
                        match table.columns.get(column) {
                            None => issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), format!("undeclared column {column}")),
                            Some(kind) if !kind.is_indexable() => issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), format!("column {column} is {kind}, which cannot be indexed")),
                            _ => {}
                        }
                    }
                    IndexComponent::Calendar(key) => {
                        if !seen.insert(&key.function) || table.columns.contains_key(&key.function) {
                            issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), format!("computed index key {} is repeated or collides with a column", key.function));
                        }
                        if let Err(message) = key.validate(table) { issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), message); }
                    }
                    IndexComponent::Contribution(maintain) => {
                        let format_valid = index.components.len() == 1 || (index.components.len() == 2
                            && table.expiry_column().is_some_and(|expiry| index.components[1].column().map(String::as_str) == Some(expiry)));
                        if index.unique || !format_valid || maintain.source != *table_name || name != &maintain.contribution_index() {
                            issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), "invalid internal contribution index".into());
                        }
                        if let Err(message) = maintain.validate_keys(table) { issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), message); }
                    }
                    IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, contract } => {
                        // One tokenizer, named: a store built under another
                        // contract would answer a different question.
                        words += 1;
                        if words > 1 {
                            issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), "an index has at most one words(..) component".into());
                        }
                        if index.unique {
                            issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), "a words(..) index cannot be unique: a row has one entry per word".into());
                        }
                        if fields.is_empty() {
                            issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), "words(..) names at least one text column".into());
                        }
                        if index.components.last().is_some_and(|last| last == component) {
                            issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), "words(..) is followed by the columns a page is ordered by, ending in id".into());
                        }
                        if contract.as_str() != Some(crate::words::CONTRACT) {
                            issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), format!("words(..) contract {contract} is not this engine's {}", crate::words::CONTRACT));
                        }
                        for field in fields {
                            match table.columns.get(field).map(ColumnType::required) {
                                Some(ColumnType::Text { .. } | ColumnType::Enum { .. }) => {}
                                Some(kind) => issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), format!("words({field}) needs a text or enum column; {field} is {kind}")),
                                None => issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), format!("words names undeclared column {field}")),
                            }
                        }
    fn parse_index(&mut self, unique: bool) -> Fallible<IndexDecl> {
        let site = self.take(if unique { "unique" } else { "by" })?.site;
        let mut explicit = None;
        if self.at_kind(Kind::Word) && self.peek_at(1).value == ":" {
            explicit = Some(self.word()?.value);
            self.take(":")?;
        }
        let mut columns = Vec::new();
        let mut words = None;
        let mut computed = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        loop {
            let component = self.word()?;
            if self.at(".") {
                return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, "table index columns cannot be reference paths", "index a column of this table; use recorded paths only on a maintain").guide("indexes"));
            }
            if component.value == "words" && self.at("(") {
                // `words(body, caption)`: one entry per distinct word of the
                // named text columns, in this position.
                if words.is_some() {
                    return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, "an index has one words(..) component", "words(body)"));
                }
                self.take("(")?;
                let mut fields = vec![self.word()?.value];
                while self.maybe(",") {
                    fields.push(self.word()?.value);
                }
                self.take(")")?;
                words = Some((columns.len(), fields));
            } else if matches!(component.value.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket" | "month" | "year") && self.at("(") {
                self.position -= 1;
                let expression = self.expression(0, &[",", "else", "<newline>"]).map_err(|error| {
                    if error.code == "E_QUARRY_MAINTAIN" { crate::diag::type_error(&error.site, error.message, "day(at), hour(at, 30m), or bucket(at, 2h, 30m); declare at: time on this table").guide("indexes") }
                    else { error }
                })?;
                if computed.insert(component.value.clone(), expression).is_some() {
                    return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, format!("index repeats {}(..); each time function occurs at most once per index", component.value), "keep one day, hour and bucket component per index; declare another index for another period").guide("indexes"));
                }
                columns.push(component.value);
            } else {
                columns.push(component.value);
            }
            if !self.maybe(",") {
                break;
            }
        }
        let explicit = explicit.or_else(|| unique.then(|| {
            let descriptor = (&columns, &words, computed.iter().map(|(name, value)| (name, crate::print::expression(value))).collect::<Vec<_>>(), true);
            let hash = snapback4_core::value::digest(&descriptor);
            format!("$intent:by{}:{}", columns.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>(), &hash[..16])
        }));
        let Some(name) = explicit else {
            let inferred = format!("by{}", columns.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>());
            return Err(syntax_with(&site, "index declarations require an explicit name", format!("{} {inferred}: {}", if unique { "unique" } else { "by" }, columns.join(", "))));
        };
        if let Some((position, fields)) = &words {
            if unique {
                return Err(syntax_with(&site, "a words(..) index cannot be unique: a row has one entry per word", format!("by {name}: {}", columns.iter().take(*position).cloned().chain(std::iter::once(format!("words({})", fields.join(", ")))).chain(columns.iter().skip(*position).cloned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
            }
            if *position == columns.len() {
                return Err(syntax_with(&site, "words(..) is followed by the columns a search page is ordered by, ending in id", format!("by {name}: {}words({}), at, id", columns.iter().map(|c| format!("{c}, ")).collect::<String>(), fields.join(", "))));
            }
        }
        let refusal = if unique && self.maybe("else") {
            let code = self.word()?.value;
            let message = if self.at_kind(Kind::Str) { Some(self.take_kind(Kind::Str)?.value) } else { None };
            Some((code, message))
        } else { None };
        self.newline()?;
        Ok(IndexDecl { name, columns, unique, refusal, words, computed, site })

codex
I found a concrete false-refusal path: strengthening an existing named nonunique index to a unique index appends the current declaration but leaves the predecessor declaration in place. With the retired table from the supplied CLI fixture, neither current-source fallback is available, so the duplicate index declaration makes deploy refuse. This also occurs when the ordered components are identical after declared identity alignment.

codex
MATERIAL — strengthening an existing named index can falsely refuse ordinary deploy.

Concrete counterexample: take the text variant of [the new CLI fixture](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/tests/roll_views.rs:28) and add this declaration to **v1**:

```quarry
  by byCustomer: code
```

Keep v2 unchanged, including `label renamed from code` and `unique byCustomer: label`.

[release_bridge_rules.rs:173](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge_rules.rs:173) appends the transported `unique byCustomer: code`. However, removal at line 90 considers only predecessor **unique** indexes, leaving the original nonunique `byCustomer` declaration. Compilation therefore reports `duplicate index items.byCustomer`.

Both current-source fallbacks fail because v2 omits predecessor table `retired`. Consequently, deploy refuses despite unchanged ordered components after identity alignment and an available truthful bridge preserving current uniqueness.

The transport must replace the predecessor’s same-named nonunique declaration when carrying this strengthening.

Static review only; no builds, tests, edits, or review files.
